
```text
ROYAL-CAPITAL-FORTIFICATION-CAD-R0A-CP2 — STRAIGHT WALL-SPAN PILOT, SEMANTIC PARTS/SOCKETS, FIXED TESSELLATION & CLEAN REPLAY
```

## R0A-CP2 implementation

Straight wall-span pilot accepted: 5 semantic parts, 10 sockets, fixed tessellation and byte-identical clean replay. Next: R0A-CP3 source coverage and negative gates.

# RC-FORT R0A-CP0 — exact build123d/OCP runtime admission

```text
source/reference fence       PASS
exact wheel delivery         58 / 58 PASS
remote fresh runtime A/B     PASS / PASS
local fresh runtime A/B      PASS / PASS
pip check                    PASS ×2
native capability            9 / 9 PASS ×2
cold A/B canonical           PASS
artifact transport           PASS
stage completion             R0A_CP0_COMPLETE / CLOSED
R0A-CP1 start                ALLOWED
```

The exact CPython 3.13.5 Linux wheelhouse, complete hash lock, delivery logs, probe artifacts and provenance are retained under `runtime/`. Extracted venvs remain outside the product tree. The first interrupted local B materialization was preserved and never reused; B was admitted in a new fresh destination.

Raw STEP bytes differ because the exporter embeds output path and timestamp in `FILE_NAME`. Only those fields are normalized for canonical comparison. Raw STEP hashes remain evidence; BREP bytes, imported geometry, volume and tessellation counts are identical.

Next: `ROYAL-CAPITAL-FORTIFICATION-CAD-R0A-CP1 — PROJECT-OWNED PROVIDER ADAPTER, NEUTRAL REQUEST/RESULT TYPES, UNIT/FRAME/TOLERANCE CONTRACT & CAD PROVIDER RECEIPT`.

## Local independent A/B


The first B materialization was interrupted by the tool timeout after creating a partial venv. It was moved to `failed_runtime_B_timeout`, recorded, and never reused. Retry B used a fresh destination.

## STEP determinism boundary

The two raw STEP files are not byte-identical because the exporter writes the output file name and current timestamp into `FILE_NAME`. Both raw hashes are preserved. The canonical digest normalizes only those fields. BREP raw bytes are identical, both STEP/BREP files reopen to the same volume, and all geometry checks match.

## Scope boundary

This closeout admits the exact CAD runtime and primitive provider capabilities only. It does not implement the project-owned adapter, wall geometry, semantic source coverage, Godot import, Manifold fallback, or product release.


# Remote Actions execution provenance

The local host remained network-isolated. A bounded GitHub Actions workflow resolved the exact CP313 wheel closure and self-admitted it in a fresh environment. The delivery upload failed at `actions/upload-artifact` because artifact storage quota was exhausted. The job run, workflow blob, commit and selected log outcomes are fixed in `remote/GITHUB_ACTIONS_RECEIPT.json`. Remote evidence is not promoted to local runtime admission.

----- BEGIN RC_K0/child_designs/fortification/r0a_cp0/tools/check_received_wheelhouse.py -----
#!/usr/bin/env python3
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse, hashlib, json, os, re, sys
from pathlib import Path

def digest(path: Path) -> str:
    h=hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open('rb') as f:
        for chunk in iter(lambda:f.read(1024*1024),b''):
            h.update(chunk)
    return h.hexdigest()

def fail(msg: str) -> None:
    raise SystemExit(msg)

def main() -> int:
    ap=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument('--delivery-root',required=True)
    ap.add_argument('--report',required=True)
    ns=ap.parse_args()
    root=Path(ns.delivery_root)
    report=Path(ns.report)
    required=['requirements-lock.txt','WHEELHOUSE.json','SHA256SUMS.txt','DELIVERY.json']
    checks=[]
    for rel in required:
        checks.append({'name':'required:'+rel,'pass':(root/rel).is_file()})
    wh=root/'wheelhouse'
    checks.append({'name':'wheelhouse directory','pass':wh.is_dir()})
    if not all(c['pass'] for c in checks):
        result={'schema':'royal-capital.fortification.received-wheelhouse-check/1','status':'FAIL','checks':checks}
        report.write_text(json.dumps(result,indent=2,sort_keys=True)+'\n',encoding='utf-8'); return 1
    manifest=json.loads((root/'WHEELHOUSE.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
    delivery=json.loads((root/'DELIVERY.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
    wheels=sorted(wh.glob('*.whl'))
    checks += [
        {'name':'wheel count 58','pass':len(wheels)==58 and manifest.get('wheel_count')==58},
        {'name':'source commit','pass':manifest.get('build123d_source_commit')=='e22d34dae17111e5b9fdb361317e055d0daae466'},
        {'name':'OCP expected hash','pass':manifest.get('ocp_expected_sha256')=='1702dfc8f5bdcb916ce65ea0c6314fe453cc78a6a3d49a044fb3062d47b0ef9f'},
    ]
    rows={r['file']:r for r in manifest.get('wheels',[])}
    checks.append({'name':'manifest wheel rows','pass':len(rows)==58})
    bad=[]
    for p in wheels:
        row=rows.get(p.name)
        if not row or int(row['bytes'])!=p.stat().st_size or row['sha256']!=digest(p):
            bad.append(p.name)
    checks.append({'name':'wheel bytes and hashes','pass':not bad,'detail':bad})
    lock_lines=[x.strip() for x in (root/'requirements-lock.txt').read_text(encoding='utf-8').splitlines() if x.strip() and not x.lstrip().startswith('#')]
    checks.append({'name':'lock rows 58','pass':len(lock_lines)==58})
    checks.append({'name':'lock all hashed','pass':all('--hash=sha256:' in x for x in lock_lines)})
    checks.append({'name':'exact OCP raw bytes','pass':any(p.name=='cadquery_ocp_novtk-8.0.1.0.0-cp313-cp313-manylinux_2_28_x86_64.whl' and digest(p)=='1702dfc8f5bdcb916ce65ea0c6314fe453cc78a6a3d49a044fb3062d47b0ef9f' for p in wheels)})
    build=[r for r in manifest.get('wheels',[]) if r.get('name','').lower()=='build123d']
    checks.append({'name':'exact source-derived build123d','pass':len(build)==1 and build[0].get('version')=='0.13.1.dev12+ge22d34dae'})
    # DELIVERY.json was written before itself; verify every listed payload entry.
    delivery_bad=[]
    for row in delivery.get('files',[]):
        rel=row.get('path','')
        if rel.startswith('/') or '..' in Path(rel).parts:
            delivery_bad.append(rel); continue
        p=root/rel
        if not p.is_file() or p.stat().st_size!=int(row['bytes']) or digest(p)!=row['sha256']:
            delivery_bad.append(rel)
    checks.append({'name':'delivery listed payload','pass':not delivery_bad,'detail':delivery_bad})
    status='PASS' if all(c['pass'] for c in checks) else 'FAIL'
    result={'schema':'royal-capital.fortification.received-wheelhouse-check/1','status':status,'checks':checks,'wheel_count':len(wheels)}
    report.write_text(json.dumps(result,indent=2,sort_keys=True)+'\n',encoding='utf-8')
    print(json.dumps(result,indent=2,sort_keys=True))
    return 0 if status=='PASS' else 1
if __name__=='__main__': raise SystemExit(main())

----- END RC_K0/child_designs/fortification/r0a_cp0/tools/check_received_wheelhouse.py -----

----- BEGIN RC_K0/child_designs/fortification/r0a_cp0/tools/check_source_api_surface.py -----
#!/usr/bin/env python3
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse, ast, json
from pathlib import Path

REQUIRED = {
 'src/build123d/operations_part.py': ['extrude','loft'],
 'src/build123d/operations_generic.py': ['sweep'],
 'src/build123d/exporters3d.py': ['export_brep','export_step'],
}
TEXT_REQUIRED = {
 'src/build123d/topology/shape_core.py': ['tessellate'],
 'src/build123d/topology/history.py': ['OCP.collections'],
}

def defs(path: Path):
    tree=ast.parse(path.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
    return {n.name for n in ast.walk(tree) if isinstance(n,(ast.FunctionDef,ast.AsyncFunctionDef,ast.ClassDef))}

def main() -> int:
    ap=argparse.ArgumentParser(); ap.add_argument('--source-root',required=True); ap.add_argument('--report',required=True); ns=ap.parse_args()
    root=Path(ns.source_root)
    checks=[]
    for rel,names in REQUIRED.items():
        p=root/rel; found=defs(p) if p.is_file() else set()
        for name in names: checks.append({'file':rel,'symbol':name,'pass':name in found})
    for rel,tokens in TEXT_REQUIRED.items():
        p=root/rel; text=p.read_text(encoding='utf-8') if p.is_file() else ''
        for token in tokens: checks.append({'file':rel,'token':token,'pass':token in text})
    result={'schema':'royal-capital.fortification.source-api-surface/1','checks':checks,'status':'PASS' if all(c['pass'] for c in checks) else 'FAIL'}
    Path(ns.report).write_text(json.dumps(result,indent=2,sort_keys=True)+'\n',encoding='utf-8')
    print(json.dumps(result,indent=2,sort_keys=True))
    return 0 if result['status']=='PASS' else 1
if __name__=='__main__': raise SystemExit(main())

----- END RC_K0/child_designs/fortification/r0a_cp0/tools/check_source_api_surface.py -----

----- BEGIN RC_K0/child_designs/fortification/r0a_cp0/tools/check_source_fence.py -----
#!/usr/bin/env python3
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse, csv, hashlib, json, zipfile
from pathlib import Path

EXPECTED = {
    'build123d': ('build123d-e22d34dae171-source.zip', '77fe1b25fc56e7d59a89b95ae3c81af2bd0acad1814669296d786291b7f325ca'),
    'cadquery': ('cadquery-c11b3f93278b-source.zip', '70ac4afc26fb105fc6cf388d8690b52372ea874078a25f6feef80120260acd0e'),
    'building_tools': ('building_tools-0216534645b1-source.zip', 'c68f4dca2bb9870c99420ff2512c240a88a6af9d508c0b34e9e79c0ba8860c72'),
}

def sha256_bytes(data: bytes) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()

def sha256_file(path: Path) -> str:
    h=hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open('rb') as f:
        for chunk in iter(lambda:f.read(1024*1024), b''): h.update(chunk)
    return h.hexdigest()

def find_member(zf: zipfile.ZipFile, internal: str) -> str:
    # GitHub source ZIPs have exactly one repository-root prefix. Compare the
    # path after that single prefix instead of suffix-matching, which would
    # confuse root README.md with nested README.md files.
    matches=[]
    for name in zf.namelist():
        if name == internal:
            matches.append(name)
            continue
        if '/' in name and name.split('/', 1)[1] == internal:
            matches.append(name)
    if len(matches) != 1:
        raise RuntimeError(f'{internal}: expected one archive-root-relative member, found {matches}')
    return matches[0]

def main() -> int:
    ap=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument('--packet-root', required=True)
    ap.add_argument('--archive-dir', required=True)
    ap.add_argument('--report', required=True)
    ns=ap.parse_args()
    root=Path(ns.packet_root)
    archive_dir=Path(ns.archive_dir)
    usage=root/'child_designs/fortification/data/OSS_SOURCE_USAGE.csv'
    rows=list(csv.DictReader(usage.open(encoding='utf-8-sig', newline='')))
    checks=[]
    archives={}
    for project,(name,want) in EXPECTED.items():
        p=archive_dir/name
        got=sha256_file(p) if p.is_file() else None
        ok=got == want
        archives[project]={'path':str(p),'expected_sha256':want,'actual_sha256':got,'present':p.is_file(),'pass':ok}
        checks.append(ok)
    by_project={p:[] for p in EXPECTED}
    for row in rows:
        project=row['project']
        if project not in by_project: continue
        by_project[project].append(row)
    selected_total=0
    selected_pass=0
    findings=[]
    for project, project_rows in by_project.items():
        archive=archive_dir/EXPECTED[project][0]
        if not archive.is_file():
            findings.append({'project':project,'error':'ARCHIVE_MISSING'})
            continue
        with zipfile.ZipFile(archive) as zf:
            for row in project_rows:
                selected_total += 1
                member=find_member(zf,row['source_internal_path'])
                raw=zf.read(member)
                selected=root/'child_designs/fortification'/row['selected_bundle_path']
                selected_raw=selected.read_bytes() if selected.is_file() else b''
                ok=(len(raw)==int(row['bytes']) and sha256_bytes(raw)==row['sha256'] and raw==selected_raw)
                selected_pass += int(ok)
                if not ok:
                    findings.append({'project':project,'source':row['source_internal_path'],'selected':str(selected),'member':member,'error':'BYTE_MISMATCH'})
    result={
        'schema':'royal-capital.fortification.source-fence-report/1',
        'archives':archives,
        'selected_files':{'expected':selected_total,'passed':selected_pass},
        'findings':findings,
        'status':'PASS' if all(checks) and selected_total==selected_pass else 'FAIL',
    }
    Path(ns.report).write_text(json.dumps(result,indent=2,sort_keys=True)+'\n',encoding='utf-8')
    print(json.dumps(result,indent=2,sort_keys=True))
    return 0 if result['status']=='PASS' else 1
if __name__=='__main__': raise SystemExit(main())

----- END RC_K0/child_designs/fortification/r0a_cp0/tools/check_source_fence.py -----

----- BEGIN RC_K0/child_designs/fortification/r0a_cp0/tools/materialize_runtime.py -----
#!/usr/bin/env python3
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse, hashlib, json, os, shutil, subprocess, sys, venv
from pathlib import Path

def sha(path: Path):
    h=hashlib.sha256();
    with path.open('rb') as f:
        for c in iter(lambda:f.read(1024*1024),b''):h.update(c)
    return h.hexdigest()

def main() -> int:
    ap=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument('--python',required=True); ap.add_argument('--wheelhouse',required=True); ap.add_argument('--lock',required=True); ap.add_argument('--destination',required=True); ap.add_argument('--receipt',required=True)
    ns=ap.parse_args(); wheelhouse=Path(ns.wheelhouse); lock=Path(ns.lock); dest=Path(ns.destination); receipt=Path(ns.receipt)
    wheels=sorted(wheelhouse.glob('*.whl'))
    lock_text=lock.read_text(encoding='utf-8') if lock.is_file() else ''
    complete=bool(wheels) and '--hash=sha256:' in lock_text and 'INCOMPLETE' not in lock_text
    if not complete:
        result={'schema':'royal-capital.fortification.runtime-admission/1','status':'BLOCKED_MISSING_EXACT_WHEELHOUSE','wheel_count':len(wheels),'lock_present':lock.is_file(),'lock_complete':False,'global_install':False}
        receipt.write_text(json.dumps(result,indent=2,sort_keys=True)+'\n',encoding='utf-8'); print(json.dumps(result,indent=2)); return 2
    if dest.exists(): raise SystemExit('destination must be fresh')
    subprocess.run([ns.python,'-m','venv',str(dest)],check=True)
    py=dest/'bin/python'
    install=subprocess.run([str(py),'-m','pip','install','--no-index','--find-links',str(wheelhouse),'--require-hashes','-r',str(lock)],capture_output=True,text=True)
    pipcheck=subprocess.run([str(py),'-m','pip','check'],capture_output=True,text=True) if install.returncode==0 else None
    result={'schema':'royal-capital.fortification.runtime-admission/1','status':'ADMITTED' if install.returncode==0 and pipcheck and pipcheck.returncode==0 else 'FAILED','global_install':False,'python':str(py),'wheelhouse':[{'name':p.name,'size':p.stat().st_size,'sha256':sha(p)} for p in wheels],'install':{'returncode':install.returncode,'stdout':install.stdout,'stderr':install.stderr},'pip_check':None if pipcheck is None else {'returncode':pipcheck.returncode,'stdout':pipcheck.stdout,'stderr':pipcheck.stderr}}
    receipt.write_text(json.dumps(result,indent=2,sort_keys=True)+'\n',encoding='utf-8'); print(json.dumps(result,indent=2)); return 0 if result['status']=='ADMITTED' else 1
if __name__=='__main__': raise SystemExit(main())

----- BEGIN RC_K0/child_designs/fortification/r0a_cp0/tools/probe_host_runtime.py -----
#!/usr/bin/env python3
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse, hashlib, importlib, importlib.metadata as md, json, platform, subprocess, sys, sysconfig, traceback
from pathlib import Path

def digest(path: Path) -> str:
    h=hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open('rb') as f:
        for c in iter(lambda:f.read(1024*1024), b''): h.update(c)
    return h.hexdigest()

def dist_version(name: str):
    try: return md.version(name)
    except md.PackageNotFoundError: return None

def main() -> int:
    ap=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument('--exact-source-root', required=True)
    ap.add_argument('--report', required=True)
    ns=ap.parse_args()
    before=subprocess.run([sys.executable,'-m','pip','list','--format=freeze'],capture_output=True,text=True,check=False)
    packages={n:dist_version(n) for n in [
        'build123d','cadquery-ocp','cadquery-ocp-novtk','cadquery','numpy','typing_extensions','svgpathtools','anytree','ezdxf','fonttools','ipython','ocpsvg','ocp_gordon','trianglesolver','sympy','scipy','scikit-learn','webcolors','requests','lib3mf','bd_materials','threejs-materials']}
    module_checks={}
    for module in ['OCP','OCP.collections','build123d']:
        try:
            importlib.import_module(module)
            module_checks[module]={'imported':True,'error':None}
        except Exception as exc:
            module_checks[module]={'imported':False,'error':f'{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}'}
    exact_import={}
    code='import build123d; print(build123d.__version__)'
    env=dict(__import__('os').environ)
    env['PYTHONPATH']=str(Path(ns.exact_source_root)/'src')
    proc=subprocess.run([sys.executable,'-c',code],capture_output=True,text=True,env=env,check=False)
    exact_import={'returncode':proc.returncode,'stdout':proc.stdout.strip(),'stderr':proc.stderr.strip()}
    after=subprocess.run([sys.executable,'-m','pip','list','--format=freeze'],capture_output=True,text=True,check=False)
    before_hash=hashlib.sha256(before.stdout.encode()).hexdigest()
    after_hash=hashlib.sha256(after.stdout.encode()).hexdigest()
    ocp_ok=packages.get('cadquery-ocp-novtk')=='8.0.1.0.0' and module_checks['OCP.collections']['imported']
    result={
      'schema':'royal-capital.fortification.host-preflight/1',
      'python':{'version':platform.python_version(),'cache_tag':sys.implementation.cache_tag,'soabi':sysconfig.get_config_var('SOABI'),'platform':sysconfig.get_platform(),'executable':sys.executable,'executable_sha256':digest(Path(sys.executable))},
      'packages':packages,
      'module_checks':module_checks,
      'exact_source_import':exact_import,
      'global_environment_unchanged':before_hash==after_hash,
      'pip_list_before_sha256':before_hash,
      'pip_list_after_sha256':after_hash,
      'exact_runtime_compatible':ocp_ok,
      'status':'BOOTSTRAP_REJECTED_VERSION_MISMATCH' if not ocp_ok else 'HOST_EXACT_RUNTIME_PRESENT',
    }
    Path(ns.report).write_text(json.dumps(result,indent=2,sort_keys=True)+'\n',encoding='utf-8')
    print(json.dumps(result,indent=2,sort_keys=True))
    return 0
if __name__=='__main__': raise SystemExit(main())

----- END RC_K0/child_designs/fortification/r0a_cp0/tools/probe_host_runtime.py -----

----- BEGIN RC_K0/child_designs/fortification/r0a_cp0/tools/run_capability_probe.py -----
#!/usr/bin/env python3
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse, hashlib, importlib.metadata, json, re
from pathlib import Path

def file_sha(path: Path) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()

def normalized_step_sha(path: Path) -> str:
    text=path.read_text(encoding="ascii")
    normalized,count=re.subn(r"FILE_NAME\(\s*'[^']*'\s*,\s*'[^']*'", "FILE_NAME('<NORMALIZED_NAME>','1970-01-01T00:00:00'", text, count=1)
    if count != 1:
        raise RuntimeError("STEP FILE_NAME header not found exactly once")
    return hashlib.sha256(normalized.replace("\r\n","\n").encode("ascii")).hexdigest()

def main() -> int:
    ap=argparse.ArgumentParser(); ap.add_argument('--output-dir',required=True); ap.add_argument('--report',required=True); ns=ap.parse_args()
    out=Path(ns.output_dir); out.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=False)
    checks={}; canonical=None; raw=None
    try:
        import build123d as b3d
        checks['import']=True
        with b3d.BuildPart() as ex:
            with b3d.BuildSketch(): b3d.Rectangle(10,6)
            b3d.extrude(amount=4)
        checks['extrude']=ex.part.volume>0
        path=b3d.Line((0,0,0),(0,0,12)); profile=b3d.Circle(1).face(); checks['sweep']=b3d.sweep(profile,path).volume>0
        with b3d.BuildPart() as lf:
            with b3d.BuildSketch(b3d.Plane.XY): b3d.Circle(2)
            with b3d.BuildSketch(b3d.Plane.XY.offset(8)): b3d.Circle(4)
            b3d.loft()
        checks['loft']=lf.part.volume>0
        a=b3d.Box(8,8,8); c=b3d.Cylinder(2,12).translate((4,4,-2)); union=a+c; cut=a-c
        checks['boolean_union']=union.volume>a.volume; checks['boolean_cut']=0<cut.volume<a.volume
        verts,tris=ex.part.tessellate(0.1); checks['tessellate']=len(verts)>0 and len(tris)>0
        step=out/'probe.step'; brep=out/'probe.brep'; b3d.export_step(ex.part,step); b3d.export_brep(ex.part,brep)
        checks['step_export_reopen']=abs(b3d.import_step(step).volume-ex.part.volume)<1e-7
        checks['brep_export_reopen']=abs(b3d.import_brep(brep).volume-ex.part.volume)<1e-7
        canonical={'build123d_version':importlib.metadata.version('build123d'),'ocp_version':importlib.metadata.version('cadquery-ocp-novtk'),'volume':round(ex.part.volume,9),'vertex_count':len(verts),'triangle_count':len(tris),'step_normalized_sha256':normalized_step_sha(step),'brep_sha256':file_sha(brep)}
        raw={'step_raw_sha256':file_sha(step),'brep_raw_sha256':file_sha(brep)}
        (out/'canonical.json').write_text(json.dumps(canonical,sort_keys=True,separators=(',',':'))+'\n',encoding='utf-8')
        status='PASS' if all(checks.values()) else 'FAIL'; error=None
    except Exception as exc:
        status='FAIL'; error=f'{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}'
    result={'schema':'royal-capital.fortification.capability-probe/2','status':status,'checks':checks,'canonical':canonical,'raw_evidence':raw,'normalization_policy':'STEP FILE_NAME name/timestamp normalized only for canonical comparison','error':error}
    Path(ns.report).write_text(json.dumps(result,indent=2,sort_keys=True)+'\n',encoding='utf-8'); print(json.dumps(result,indent=2)); return 0 if status=='PASS' else 1
if __name__=='__main__': raise SystemExit(main())

----- END RC_K0/child_designs/fortification/r0a_cp0/tools/run_capability_probe.py -----

#!/usr/bin/env python3
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse, hashlib, json, shutil, subprocess
from pathlib import Path

def sha(p: Path): return hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
def main() -> int:
    ap=argparse.ArgumentParser(); ap.add_argument('--runtime-python',required=True); ap.add_argument('--work-root',required=True); ap.add_argument('--report',required=True); ns=ap.parse_args()
    root=Path(ns.work_root)
    if root.exists(): raise SystemExit('cold A/B root must be fresh')
    script=Path(__file__).with_name('run_capability_probe.py')
    runs=[]
    for label in ['A','B']:
        d=root/label; report=d/'report.json'; d.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
        proc=subprocess.run([ns.runtime_python,str(script),'--output-dir',str(d/'output'),'--report',str(report)],capture_output=True,text=True)
        runs.append({'label':label,'returncode':proc.returncode,'report':json.loads(report.read_text()) if report.exists() else None,'canonical_sha256':sha(d/'output/canonical.json') if (d/'output/canonical.json').exists() else None,'stdout':proc.stdout,'stderr':proc.stderr})
    identical=runs[0]['returncode']==0 and runs[1]['returncode']==0 and runs[0]['canonical_sha256']==runs[1]['canonical_sha256']
    result={'schema':'royal-capital.fortification.cold-ab/1','status':'PASS' if identical else 'FAIL','byte_identical_canonical':identical,'runs':runs}
    Path(ns.report).write_text(json.dumps(result,indent=2,sort_keys=True)+'\n',encoding='utf-8'); print(json.dumps(result,indent=2)); return 0 if identical else 1
if __name__=='__main__': raise SystemExit(main())

#!/usr/bin/env python3
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse, csv, hashlib, json, stat
from pathlib import Path

EXPECTED_OCP='1702dfc8f5bdcb916ce65ea0c6314fe453cc78a6a3d49a044fb3062d47b0ef9f'
EXPECTED_SOURCE='e22d34dae17111e5b9fdb361317e055d0daae466'
EXPECTED_BUILD123D='0.13.1.dev12+ge22d34dae'

def load(path: Path):
    return json.loads(path.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))

def sha(path: Path):
    h=hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open('rb') as f:
        for chunk in iter(lambda:f.read(1024*1024),b''): h.update(chunk)
    return h.hexdigest()

def main() -> int:
    ap=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument('--cp0-root',required=True)
    ap.add_argument('--report',required=True)
    ns=ap.parse_args(); root=Path(ns.cp0_root); report=Path(ns.report)
    checks=[]
    def add(name,passed,detail=None):
        row={'name':name,'pass':bool(passed)}
        if detail is not None: row['detail']=detail
        checks.append(row)

    required=[
      'config/PYTHON_ABI.json','config/SOURCE_ARCHIVES.json','config/OCP_WHEEL_RECEIPT.json','config/REQUIRED_CAPABILITIES.json',
      'runtime/requirements-lock.txt','runtime/WHEELHOUSE.json','runtime/SHA256SUMS.txt','runtime/DELIVERY.json','runtime/WHEEL_LOCK_STATUS.json',
      'reports/source_fence.json','reports/source_api_surface.json','reports/host_runtime_preflight.json',
      'reports/runtime_admission.json','reports/capability_preflight.json','reports/cp0_status.json',
      'reports/resume3/received_wheelhouse.json','reports/resume3/runtime_admission_A.json','reports/resume3/runtime_admission_B.json',
      'reports/resume3/capability_A.json','reports/resume3/capability_B.json','reports/resume3/local_cold_ab.json',
      'remote/GITHUB_ACTIONS_RECEIPT.json','remote/REMOTE_DELIVERY_RECEIPT.json','remote/REMOTE_CAPABILITY_PROBE.json',
      'remote/TRANSPORT_BLOCKER.json','remote/RUN_HISTORY.json','provenance/EXTERNAL_ARTIFACTS.csv','provenance/CHANGESET.json',
      'docs/CP0_CLOSEOUT.md'
    ]
    for rel in required: add('required:'+rel,(root/rel).is_file())
    if not all(c['pass'] for c in checks):
        result={'schema':'royal-capital.fortification.cp0-final-validation/2','status':'FAIL_REQUIRED_FILES','checks':checks,'passed':sum(c['pass'] for c in checks),'total':len(checks)}
        report.write_text(json.dumps(result,indent=2,sort_keys=True)+'\n'); print(json.dumps(result,indent=2)); return 1

    source=load(root/'reports/source_fence.json'); api=load(root/'reports/source_api_surface.json')
    host=load(root/'reports/host_runtime_preflight.json'); received=load(root/'reports/resume3/received_wheelhouse.json')
    wh=load(root/'runtime/WHEELHOUSE.json'); delivery=load(root/'runtime/DELIVERY.json'); wheel_status=load(root/'runtime/WHEEL_LOCK_STATUS.json')
    runtime=load(root/'reports/runtime_admission.json'); cap=load(root/'reports/capability_preflight.json'); status=load(root/'reports/cp0_status.json')
    A=load(root/'reports/resume3/runtime_admission_A.json'); B=load(root/'reports/resume3/runtime_admission_B.json')
    capA=load(root/'reports/resume3/capability_A.json'); capB=load(root/'reports/resume3/capability_B.json'); cold=load(root/'reports/resume3/local_cold_ab.json')
    remote=load(root/'remote/REMOTE_CAPABILITY_PROBE.json'); action=load(root/'remote/GITHUB_ACTIONS_RECEIPT.json')
    transport=load(root/'remote/TRANSPORT_BLOCKER.json'); ocp_receipt=load(root/'config/OCP_WHEEL_RECEIPT.json'); required_caps=load(root/'config/REQUIRED_CAPABILITIES.json')

    add('source fence',source.get('status')=='PASS' and source.get('selected_files')=={'expected':105,'passed':105})
    add('source API surface',api.get('status')=='PASS' and len(api.get('checks',[]))==7 and all(x.get('pass') for x in api.get('checks',[])))
    add('bootstrap mismatch kept explicit',host.get('exact_runtime_compatible') is False and host.get('status')=='BOOTSTRAP_REJECTED_VERSION_MISMATCH')
    add('wheel intake checker',received.get('status')=='PASS' and received.get('wheel_count')==58 and all(x.get('pass') for x in received.get('checks',[])))
    add('wheel manifest identity',wh.get('wheel_count')==58 and wh.get('build123d_source_commit')==EXPECTED_SOURCE and wh.get('ocp_expected_sha256')==EXPECTED_OCP and wh.get('python_observed')=='3.13.5')
    wheel_rows=wh.get('wheels',[]); wheel_dir=root/'runtime/wheelhouse'
    actual=list(sorted(wheel_dir.glob('*.whl')))
    add('wheel files exactly 58',len(actual)==58 and len(wheel_rows)==58)
    expected_by_file={x['file']:x for x in wheel_rows}
    mismatches=[]
    for p in actual:
        row=expected_by_file.get(p.name)
        if row is None or p.stat().st_size!=row.get('bytes') or sha(p)!=row.get('sha256'): mismatches.append(p.name)
    add('all wheel bytes match manifest',not mismatches and set(expected_by_file)=={p.name for p in actual},mismatches)
    ocp_file=wheel_dir/'cadquery_ocp_novtk-8.0.1.0.0-cp313-cp313-manylinux_2_28_x86_64.whl'
    build_file=wheel_dir/'build123d-0.13.1.dev12+ge22d34dae-py3-none-any.whl'
    add('exact OCP raw bytes',ocp_file.is_file() and sha(ocp_file)==EXPECTED_OCP)
    add('exact source-derived build123d wheel',build_file.is_file() and next((x for x in wheel_rows if x.get('name')=='build123d'),{}).get('version')==EXPECTED_BUILD123D)
    add('delivery manifest complete',delivery.get('status')=='BUILT_SELF_ADMITTED_COLD_AB_PASS' and delivery.get('wheel_count')==58)
    add('transport blocker resolved',transport.get('status')=='RESOLVED' and transport.get('local_intake')=='PASS')
    add('remote cold A/B',remote.get('status')=='PASS_REMOTE_COLD_AB' and remote.get('cold_ab',{}).get('status')=='PASS' and remote.get('cold_ab',{}).get('byte_identical_canonical') is True)
    add('remote workflow success',action.get('conclusion')=='success' and action.get('resolved_wheels')==58 and all(s.get('conclusion')=='success' for s in action.get('steps',[])))

    for label,receipt in [('A',A),('B',B)]:
        add(f'local runtime {label} admitted',receipt.get('status')=='ADMITTED' and receipt.get('global_install') is False and len(receipt.get('wheelhouse',[]))==58)
        add(f'local runtime {label} install pass',receipt.get('install',{}).get('returncode')==0)
        add(f'local runtime {label} pip check pass',(receipt.get('pip_check') or {}).get('returncode')==0)
    for label,probe in [('A',capA),('B',capB)]:
        add(f'local capability {label} 9/9',probe.get('status')=='PASS' and len(probe.get('checks',{}))==9 and all(probe.get('checks',{}).values()))
        add(f'local capability {label} exact versions',probe.get('canonical',{}).get('build123d_version')==EXPECTED_BUILD123D and probe.get('canonical',{}).get('ocp_version')=='8.0.1.0.0')
    add('local cold A/B pass',cold.get('status')=='PASS' and cold.get('canonical',{}).get('byte_identical') is True)
    add('raw BREP byte identity',cold.get('canonical',{}).get('raw_brep_byte_identical') is True)
    add('raw STEP metadata difference explicit',cold.get('canonical',{}).get('raw_step_byte_identical') is False and 'STEP FILE_NAME' in cold.get('canonical',{}).get('comparison_policy',''))
    add('global environment unchanged',cold.get('global_environment',{}).get('unchanged') is True and cold.get('global_environment',{}).get('global_install') is False)
    add('partial timeout preserved',cold.get('interrupted_attempt',{}).get('status')=='PRESERVED_PARTIAL_TIMEOUT' and cold.get('interrupted_attempt',{}).get('source_preservation')=='PASS')
    add('runtime admission promoted',runtime.get('status')=='ADMITTED' and runtime.get('promotion_allowed') is True and runtime.get('local_cold_ab')=='PASS')
    add('capability preflight promoted',cap.get('status')=='PASS_LOCAL_COLD_AB' and cap.get('promotion_allowed') is True and cap.get('local_native_capabilities')=='9_OF_9_PASS_X2')
    add('wheel lock admitted',wheel_status.get('status')=='ADMITTED_LOCAL_COLD_AB' and wheel_status.get('blocking_reason') is None)
    add('OCP receipt admitted',ocp_receipt.get('admission_state')=='ADMITTED_LOCAL_COLD_AB' and ocp_receipt.get('raw_bytes_state')=='PRESENT_EXACT')
    add('required capabilities executed',required_caps.get('current_execution_state')=='PASS_LOCAL_COLD_AB' and required_caps.get('cold_ab_canonical_equality')=='PASS')
    add('CP0 closed and CP1 allowed',status.get('stage_completion')=='R0A_CP0_COMPLETE' and status.get('functional_qualification')=='PASS' and status.get('r0a_cp1_start_allowed') is True)

    # Product tree hygiene: runtime wheels are retained; extracted venv/cache artifacts are not.
    forbidden=[]
    for p in root.rglob('*'):
        rel_path=p.relative_to(root)
        rel=rel_path.as_posix()
        directory_parts=rel_path.parts if p.is_dir() else rel_path.parts[:-1]
        extracted_runtime_dir=any(part in {'runtime_A','runtime_B','runtime_B_retry1','failed_runtime_B_timeout'} for part in directory_parts)
        if '__pycache__' in rel_path.parts or p.suffix in {'.pyc','.pyo'} or '.godot' in rel_path.parts or extracted_runtime_dir:
            forbidden.append(rel)
    add('product tree excludes extracted runtimes and caches',not forbidden,forbidden[:20])

    result={
      'schema':'royal-capital.fortification.cp0-final-validation/2',
      'status':'PASS_R0A_CP0_COMPLETE' if all(c['pass'] for c in checks) else 'FAIL',
      'checks':checks,'passed':sum(c['pass'] for c in checks),'total':len(checks),
      'source_preservation':'PASS','functional_qualification':'PASS' if all(c['pass'] for c in checks) else 'FAIL',
      'stage_completion':'R0A_CP0_COMPLETE' if all(c['pass'] for c in checks) else 'HOLD',
      'r0a_cp1_start_allowed':all(c['pass'] for c in checks),
    }
    report.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    report.write_text(json.dumps(result,indent=2,sort_keys=True)+'\n',encoding='utf-8')
    print(json.dumps(result,indent=2,sort_keys=True))
    return 0 if result['status'].startswith('PASS') else 1
if __name__=='__main__': raise SystemExit(main())

----- END RC_K0/child_designs/fortification/r0a_cp0/tools/validate_cp0.py -----

----- BEGIN RC_K0/child_designs/fortification/r0a_cp1/README.md -----
# R0A-CP1 — project-owned build123d provider adapter

이 디렉터리는 build123d/OCP를 제품 계약 뒤에 격리하는 얇은 project-owned adapter를 보존한다.

```text
public input       neutral JSON/dataclass
provider interior  build123d/OCP
public result      neutral indexed mesh + stored-copy refs + typed status
receipt            source/runtime/unit/frame/tolerance/output digests
```

현재 구현 범위는 `PROFILE_EXTRUSION` 한 종류다. 이는 CP2 straight wall pilot이 사용할 최소 형상 경계이며, 도시 배치·성벽 의미·LOD·Godot scene은 소유하지 않는다.

----- END RC_K0/child_designs/fortification/r0a_cp1/README.md -----

----- BEGIN RC_K0/child_designs/fortification/r0a_cp1/docs/CP1_REPORT.md -----
# ROYAL-CAPITAL-FORTIFICATION-CAD-R0A-CP1 완료 보고서

```text
source preservation          PASS
functional qualification     PASS
stage completion             R0A_CP1_COMPLETE / CLOSED
next                          R0A-CP2
```

## 구현

- `PROFILE_EXTRUSION` neutral request와 result dataclass/JSON 계약
- 프로젝트 `METER`, `+Y up`, `-Z forward` → provider `MILLIMETER`, `+Z up` 변환
- determinant +1 orientation과 1000:1 length scale
- model/tessellation tolerance domain
- typed reject/fail normalization과 required failure 시 geometry 미게시
- provider tessellation을 canonical neutral indexed mesh로 변환
- STEP/BREP memory export, byte hash, reopen volume 검증
- source/runtime/contract/output digest가 포함된 `CADProviderReceipt`
- public annotation과 초기 package import에서 build123d/OCP type 비노출

## 검증

```text
fresh CP0 runtime materialization  PASS
qualification fixture              PASS
negative fixtures                   5 / 5 PASS
unittest                            4 / 4 PASS
CLI smoke                           PASS
public API audit                    PASS
cold A/B files                      5 / 5 BYTE-IDENTICAL
neutral mesh                        8 vertices / 12 triangles
volume                              48.0 m³
STEP/BREP reopen                    48.0 m³ / 48.0 m³
```

## 발견·수정 기록

1. 최초 extrusion call이 `amount` 없이 `dir`만 전달되어 provider가 거부했다. 실패 결과를 보존하고 explicit amount+unit direction으로 수정했다.
2. project meter 값을 provider numeric meter로 직접 사용하고 STEP `Unit.M`으로 내보내면 build123d reopen이 millimeter 숫자로 변환됐다. adapter 경계를 meter→millimeter 1000:1로 명시해 수정했다.
3. 초기 public-type 검사가 provider 이름과 neutral field name의 `build123d` 문자열까지 type leakage로 오판했다. 실제 resolved annotation module을 검사하도록 validator를 교정했다.

## 비범위

straight wall 의미 부품, socket, source surface coverage, LOD, Godot는 CP2 이후다.

----- END RC_K0/child_designs/fortification/r0a_cp1/docs/CP1_REPORT.md -----
